### tests/operations/pos20-control-plane.test.mjs stage 2 earlier PRs
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import {decide,evidenceDebt} from '../../scripts/pos20-cognitive-controller.mjs';
import {runBenchmark} from '../../scripts/pos20-benchmark.mjs';
import {validateCurrentState,assessContinuity} from '../../scripts/pos20-recovery.mjs';
import {evaluateMerge,requiredChecks} from '../../scripts/pos20-publication.mjs';
const a='a'.repeat(40);
const baseState=()=>({objective:'Advance product',evidenceFingerprint:'e1',liveAuthorityResolved:true,headsMatchExpectation:true,processAuthoritySufficient:false,ownerRequestsTransfer:false,reviewsClean:false,recoveryState:'RECOVERY_READY',candidateValidation:'NOT_PUBLISHED',hypothesisHistory:[]});
const action=(id,x={})=>({id,productProgressPotential:.5,blockerReduction:.5,evidenceGain:.5,riskReduction:.5,automationLeverage:.5,informationGain:.5,proofCost:.2,ownerInteractionCost:.1,uncertainty:.2,...x});
test('benchmark is a hard 20/20 self-veto with >=25 point advantage',()=>{const r=runBenchmark();assert.equal(r.correct,20);assert.equal(r.score,100);assert.equal(r.baselineScore,70);assert.ok(r.advantagePoints>=25);assert.equal(r.pass,true);});
test('unsafe billing and fabricated credit are unselectable',()=>{for(const bad of [action('bad',{requiresBilling:true,productProgressPotential:1}),action('bad',{fabricatesProductCredit:true,productProgressPotential:1})])assert.equal(decide(baseState(),[bad,action('safe')]).actionId,'safe');});
test('owner priority strongly influences safe utility',()=>{const s={...baseState(),ownerPriorityActionId:'owner'};assert.equal(decide(s,[action('other',{productProgressPotential:1}),action('owner',{productProgressPotential:.2})]).actionId,'owner');});
test('owner priority cannot override permanent safety',()=>{const s={...baseState(),ownerPriorityActionId:'owner'};assert.equal(decide(s,[action('owner',{requiresBilling:true}),action('safe')]).actionId,'safe');});
test('process work is penalized after process authority is sufficient',()=>{const s={...baseState(),processAuthoritySufficient:true};assert.equal(decide(s,[action('process',{processOnly:true,productProgressPotential:.9}),action('product',{productProgressPotential:.6})]).actionId,'product');});
test('two failed attempts on unchanged evidence force reframe',()=>{const s={...baseState(),hypothesisHistory:[{hypothesisId:'h',outcome:'failed',evidenceFingerprint:'e1'},{hypothesisId:'h',outcome:'failed',evidenceFingerprint:'e1'}]};assert.equal(decide(s,[action('retry',{hypothesisId:'h'}),action('REFRAME_HYPOTHESIS')]).actionId,'REFRAME_HYPOTHESIS');});
test('materially new evidence can reopen a prior hypothesis',()=>{const s={...baseState(),evidenceFingerprint:'e2',hypothesisHistory:[{hypothesisId:'h',outcome:'failed',evidenceFingerprint:'e1'},{hypothesisId:'h',outcome:'failed',evidenceFingerprint:'e1'}]};assert.equal(decide(s,[action('retry',{hypothesisId:'h',evidenceGain:1}),action('REFRAME_HYPOTHESIS',{informationGain:.1})]).actionId,'retry');});
test('continuity hard rules outrank utility',()=>{assert.equal(decide({...baseState(),liveAuthorityResolved:false},[action('x')]).actionId,'RESOLVE_LIVE_AUTHORITY');assert.equal(decide({...baseState(),recoveryState:'RECOVERY_STALE'},[action('x')]).actionId,'REFRESH_RECOVERY');assert.equal(decide({...baseState(),headsMatchExpectation:false},[action('x')]).actionId,'RECONCILE_HEADS');});
test('candidate validation freezes mutation and exact green candidate advances to merge',()=>{assert.equal(decide({...baseState(),candidateValidation:'PENDING'},[action('x')]).actionId,'WAIT_VALIDATION');assert.equal(decide({...baseState(),candidateValidation:'FAILED'},[action('x')]).actionId,'INSPECT_EXACT_FAILURES');assert.equal(decide({...baseState(),candidateValidation:'GREEN',reviewsClean:true},[action('x')]).actionId,'MERGE_EXPECTED_HEAD');});
test('evidence debt is explicit and blocking debt is counted',()=>{assert.deepEqual(evidenceDebt({missingEvidence:[{id:'a',blocking:true},{id:'b',blocking:false}]}),{count:2,blocking:1,items:['a','b']});});
test('current state validates and retired handoff percentage is forbidden',()=>{const s=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('POS20_CURRENT_STATE.json','utf8'));assert.equal(validateCurrentState(s).operatingSystem,'POS20');assert.throws(()=>validateCurrentState({...s,selectedAction:'Report handoff proximity now'}),/retired/);});
test('categorical continuity protects recovery and pending validation',()=>{const s={continuityState:'READY',ownerRequestsTransfer:false,liveAuthorityResolved:true,durableCheckpoint:true,headsMatchExpectation:true,recoveryDescendsFromCandidate:true,openAtomicUnits:1,unpublishedPackets:0,candidateValidation:'NOT_PUBLISHED'};assert.equal(assessContinuity(s).decision,'CONTINUE');assert.equal(assessContinuity({...s,continuityState:'STALE'}).decision,'TRANSITION');assert.equal(assessContinuity({...s,candidateValidation:'PENDING'}).action,'WAIT_VALIDATION');});
test('publication requires every POS20 exact-head check',()=>{const route={operations:true,tests:['t'],proofGroups:['REMOTE']};const names=requiredChecks(route);const input={action:'MERGE',candidateHead:a,expectedHead:a,continuityState:'READY',candidateValidation:'GREEN',checkRuns:names.map(name=>({name,head:a,status:'completed',conclusion:'success'})),reviews:{head:a,changeRequests:0,unresolvedThreads:0,commentsReviewed:true}};assert.equal(evaluateMerge(input,route).expected_head_sha,a);for(const mutate of [x=>x.checkRuns.pop(),x=>x.checkRuns[0].head='b'.repeat(40),x=>x.reviews.unresolvedThreads=1,x=>x.expectedHead='b'.repeat(40)]){const bad=structuredClone(input);mutate(bad);assert.throws(()=>evaluateMerge(bad,route));}});
test('machine authority gives POS20 zero product credit and pins cognitive loop',()=>{const a=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('PROJECT_OPERATING_SYSTEM_POS20.json','utf8'));assert.deepEqual(a.productCredit,{SSJR:0,MDP:0});assert.deepEqual(a.controlLoop,['OBSERVE','MODEL','HYPOTHESIZE','PLAN','ACT','VERIFY','LEARN','RECOVER']);assert.equal(a.hardRules.fakeProductCreditForbidden,true);});
test('retired concepts include handoff percentage and recursive handoff authority',()=>{const r=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('POS20_RETIRED_CONCEPTS.json','utf8'));assert.ok(r.retired.some(x=>/Handoff proximity/i.test(x)));assert.ok(r.retired.some(x=>/Recursive handoff/i.test(x)));});
test('kernel baseline pins proven POS10 executable safety mechanisms',()=>{const k=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('POS20_KERNEL_BASELINE.json','utf8'));assert.equal(k.kernel,'POS10');assert.equal(Object.keys(k.gitBlobShas).length,3);assert.ok(k.kernelGuarantees.includes('expected-head merge protection'));});

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import {decide,evidenceDebt} from '../../scripts/pos20-cognitive-controller.mjs';
import {runBenchmark} from '../../scripts/pos20-benchmark.mjs';
import {validateCurrentState,assessContinuity} from '../../scripts/pos20-recovery.mjs';
import {evaluateMerge,requiredChecks} from '../../scripts/pos20-publication.mjs';
const a='a'.repeat(40);
const baseState=()=>({objective:'Advance product',evidenceFingerprint:'e1',liveAuthorityResolved:true,headsMatchExpectation:true,processAuthoritySufficient:false,ownerRequestsTransfer:false,reviewsClean:false,recoveryState:'RECOVERY_READY',candidateValidation:'NOT_PUBLISHED',hypothesisHistory:[]});
const action=(id,x={})=>({id,productProgressPotential:.5,blockerReduction:.5,evidenceGain:.5,riskReduction:.5,automationLeverage:.5,informationGain:.5,proofCost:.2,ownerInteractionCost:.1,uncertainty:.2,...x});
test('benchmark is a hard 20/20 self-veto with >=25 point advantage',()=>{const r=runBenchmark();assert.equal(r.correct,20);assert.equal(r.score,100);assert.equal(r.baselineScore,70);assert.ok(r.advantagePoints>=25);assert.equal(r.pass,true);});
test('unsafe billing and fabricated credit are unselectable',()=>{for(const bad of [action('bad',{requiresBilling:true,productProgressPotential:1}),action('bad',{fabricatesProductCredit:true,productProgressPotential:1})])assert.equal(decide(baseState(),[bad,action('safe')]).actionId,'safe');});
test('owner priority strongly influences safe utility',()=>{const s={...baseState(),ownerPriorityActionId:'owner'};assert.equal(decide(s,[action('other',{productProgressPotential:1}),action('owner',{productProgressPotential:.2})]).actionId,'owner');});
test('owner priority cannot override permanent safety',()=>{const s={...baseState(),ownerPriorityActionId:'owner'};assert.equal(decide(s,[action('owner',{requiresBilling:true}),action('safe')]).actionId,'safe');});
test('process work is penalized after process authority is sufficient',()=>{const s={...baseState(),processAuthoritySufficient:true};assert.equal(decide(s,[action('process',{processOnly:true,productProgressPotential:.9}),action('product',{productProgressPotential:.6})]).actionId,'product');});
test('two failed attempts on unchanged evidence force reframe',()=>{const s={...baseState(),hypothesisHistory:[{hypothesisId:'h',outcome:'failed',evidenceFingerprint:'e1'},{hypothesisId:'h',outcome:'failed',evidenceFingerprint:'e1'}]};assert.equal(decide(s,[action('retry',{hypothesisId:'h'}),action('REFRAME_HYPOTHESIS')]).actionId,'REFRAME_HYPOTHESIS');});
test('materially new evidence can reopen a prior hypothesis',()=>{const s={...baseState(),evidenceFingerprint:'e2',hypothesisHistory:[{hypothesisId:'h',outcome:'failed',evidenceFingerprint:'e1'},{hypothesisId:'h',outcome:'failed',evidenceFingerprint:'e1'}]};assert.equal(decide(s,[action('retry',{hypothesisId:'h',evidenceGain:1}),action('REFRAME_HYPOTHESIS',{informationGain:.1})]).actionId,'retry');});
test('continuity hard rules outrank utility',()=>{assert.equal(decide({...baseState(),liveAuthorityResolved:false},[action('x')]).actionId,'RESOLVE_LIVE_AUTHORITY');assert.equal(decide({...baseState(),recoveryState:'RECOVERY_STALE'},[action('x')]).actionId,'REFRESH_RECOVERY');assert.equal(decide({...baseState(),headsMatchExpectation:false},[action('x')]).actionId,'RECONCILE_HEADS');});
test('candidate validation freezes mutation and exact green candidate advances to merge',()=>{assert.equal(decide({...baseState(),candidateValidation:'PENDING'},[action('x')]).actionId,'WAIT_VALIDATION');assert.equal(decide({...baseState(),candidateValidation:'FAILED'},[action('x')]).actionId,'INSPECT_EXACT_FAILURES');assert.equal(decide({...baseState(),candidateValidation:'GREEN',reviewsClean:true},[action('x')]).actionId,'MERGE_EXPECTED_HEAD');});
test('evidence debt is explicit and blocking debt is counted',()=>{assert.deepEqual(evidenceDebt({missingEvidence:[{id:'a',blocking:true},{id:'b',blocking:false}]}),{count:2,blocking:1,items:['a','b']});});
test('current state validates and retired handoff percentage is forbidden',()=>{const s=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('POS20_CURRENT_STATE.json','utf8'));assert.equal(validateCurrentState(s).operatingSystem,'POS20');assert.throws(()=>validateCurrentState({...s,selectedAction:'Report handoff proximity now'}),/retired/);});
test('categorical continuity protects recovery and pending validation',()=>{const s={continuityState:'READY',ownerRequestsTransfer:false,liveAuthorityResolved:true,durableCheckpoint:true,headsMatchExpectation:true,recoveryDescendsFromCandidate:true,openAtomicUnits:1,unpublishedPackets:0,candidateValidation:'NOT_PUBLISHED'};assert.equal(assessContinuity(s).decision,'CONTINUE');assert.equal(assessContinuity({...s,continuityState:'STALE'}).decision,'TRANSITION');assert.equal(assessContinuity({...s,candidateValidation:'PENDING'}).action,'WAIT_VALIDATION');});
test('publication requires every POS20 exact-head check',()=>{const route={operations:true,tests:['t'],proofGroups:['REMOTE']};const names=requiredChecks(route);const input={action:'MERGE',candidateHead:a,expectedHead:a,continuityState:'READY',candidateValidation:'GREEN',checkRuns:names.map(name=>({name,head:a,status:'completed',conclusion:'success'})),reviews:{head:a,changeRequests:0,unresolvedThreads:0,commentsReviewed:true}};assert.equal(evaluateMerge(input,route).expected_head_sha,a);for(const mutate of [x=>x.checkRuns.pop(),x=>x.checkRuns[0].head='b'.repeat(40),x=>x.reviews.unresolvedThreads=1,x=>x.expectedHead='b'.repeat(40)]){const bad=structuredClone(input);mutate(bad);assert.throws(()=>evaluateMerge(bad,route));}});
test('machine authority gives POS20 zero product credit and pins cognitive loop',()=>{const a=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('PROJECT_OPERATING_SYSTEM_POS20.json','utf8'));assert.deepEqual(a.productCredit,{SSJR:0,MDP:0});assert.deepEqual(a.controlLoop,['OBSERVE','MODEL','HYPOTHESIZE','PLAN','ACT','VERIFY','LEARN','RECOVER']);assert.equal(a.hardRules.fakeProductCreditForbidden,true);});
test('retired concepts include handoff percentage and recursive handoff authority',()=>{const r=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('POS20_RETIRED_CONCEPTS.json','utf8'));assert.ok(r.retired.some(x=>/Handoff proximity/i.test(x)));assert.ok(r.retired.some(x=>/Recursive handoff/i.test(x)));});
test('kernel baseline pins proven POS10 executable safety mechanisms',()=>{const k=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('POS20_KERNEL_BASELINE.json','utf8'));assert.equal(k.kernel,'POS10');assert.equal(Object.keys(k.gitBlobShas).length,3);assert.ok(k.kernelGuarantees.includes('expected-head merge protection'));});

const expectedFullTestCount=new Set([...frozenProductManifest.tests,...supplementalPaths]).size;

test('POS20 accepts low-risk inherited routing without reducing it',()=>{const r=routePos20(['README.md']);assert.equal(r.model,'POS20');assert.equal(r.profile,'POS20_DOC_ONLY');assert.equal(r.cognitiveEscalation,false);});
test('POS20 preserves transitive product consumers from the POS10 kernel',()=>{const r=routePos20(['js/stage4ConnectedRivalry.js']);for(const id of ['PAIRING_RIVALRY','REMOTE_JOINING','SHARED_SETUP'])assert.ok(r.invariants.includes(id));});
test('POS20 supplemental registry owns all registered contracts above the frozen POS10 floor',()=>{assert.equal(supplementalRegistry.operatingSystem,'POS20');assert.deepEqual(supplementalPaths,expectedSupplementalContracts);for(const contract of supplementalPaths)assert.equal(frozenProductManifest.tests.includes(contract),false,contract);});
test('POS20 adds the registered SSJR safe-evidence contract for every owned artifact',()=>{for(const file of ['js/ssjrProductionNegativeEvidence.js','scripts/assemble-ssjr-shared-setup-safe-evidence.mjs','tests/contracts/ssjr-production-safe-evidence-assembly-contracts.cjs','POS20_SUPPLEMENTAL_PRODUCT_TESTS.json']){const r=routePos20([file]);assert.ok(r.tests.includes(safeEvidenceContract),file);assert.ok(r.testsAddedByPos20.includes(safeEvidenceContract),file);assert.equal(r.testCount,r.tests.length);}});
test('unrelated docs do not accidentally select the SSJR supplemental contract',()=>{const routed=routePos20(['README.md']).tests;for(const contract of expectedSupplementalContracts)assert.equal(routed.includes(contract),false,contract);});
test('every full seal includes the complete supplemental registry, regardless of changed files',()=>{for(const context of [{forceFull:true},{uncertainty:'HIGH'},{evidenceDebt:3},{repeatedFailure:true},{authorityChange:true}]){const r=routePos20(['README.md'],context);assert.equal(r.fullSeal,true);for(const entry of supplementalRegistry.tests)assert.ok(r.tests.includes(entry.path),entry.path);assert.equal(r.testCount,expectedFullTestCount);}const inherited=routePos20(['unregistered-runtime-file.js']);assert.equal(inherited.fullSeal,true);for(const entry of supplementalRegistry.tests)assert.ok(inherited.tests.includes(entry.path),entry.path);});
test('high uncertainty escalates to a complete inherited seal',()=>{const r=routePos20(['README.md'],{uncertainty:'HIGH'});assert.equal(r.profile,'COGNITIVE_FULL_SEAL');assert.equal(r.fullSeal,true);});
test('evidence debt or repeated failure can escalate proof confidence',()=>{assert.equal(routePos20(['README.md'],{evidenceDebt:3}).fullSeal,true);assert.equal(routePos20(['README.md'],{repeatedFailure:true}).fullSeal,true);});
const repoFixture=fs.existsSync('.git')&&fs.existsSync('CURRENT_PRODUCT_GUARDS.json');
test('POS20 preserves the pinned executable POS10 safety kernel byte-for-byte',{skip:!repoFixture},()=>{const baseline=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('POS20_KERNEL_BASELINE.json','utf8'));for(const [file,sha] of Object.entries(baseline.gitBlobShas)){const actual=execFileSync('git',['hash-object',file],{encoding:'utf8'}).trim();assert.equal(actual,sha,file);}});
test('permanent provider and product safety guards remain intact',{skip:!repoFixture},()=>{const g=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('CURRENT_PRODUCT_GUARDS.json','utf8'));assert.equal(g.provider.billingEnabled,false);assert.equal(g.provider.firebasePlan,'Spark');assert.equal(g.provider.blazeAllowed,false);assert.equal(g.provider.cloudRunAllowed,false);assert.equal(g.provider.cloudFunctionsAllowed,false);assert.equal(g.product.managerCount,2);assert.equal(g.product.pairingAndExactActiveBeforeLeagueOrClubs,true);assert.equal(g.product.candidateCOnlyDestructiveRemoteToLocalApply,true);assert.equal(g.privacy.publicDiscovery,false);assert.equal(g.privacy.publicMatchmaking,false);});
const workflow=fs.readFileSync('.github/workflows/validate-pos10.yml','utf8');
test('POS20 workflow owns benchmark and exact-head seal',()=>{assert.match(workflow,/name: POS20 cognitive benchmark/);assert.match(workflow,/name: POS20 exact-head cognitive seal/);assert.match(workflow,/scripts\/pos20-impact-router\.mjs/);});
test('main push independently forces the complete inherited seal',()=>{assert.match(workflow,/github\.event_name.*push/);assert.match(workflow,/--force-full/);});
test('all validation jobs checkout the exact candidate or push head',()=>{const hits=workflow.match(/ref: \$\{\{ github\.event\.pull_request\.head\.sha \|\| github\.sha \}\}/g)||[];assert.ok(hits.length>=5);});
test('summary rejects failed required lanes rather than treating skipped as success',()=>{assert.match(workflow,/Required POS20 lane did not pass/);assert.match(workflow,/check_lane operations/);assert.match(workflow,/check_lane deterministic/);assert.match(workflow,/check_lane proofs/);});
